# T20 quilt-to-whole recovery census

**Current poem-locator return, 2026-09-08:** all23 complete poems match their canonical source and now resolve through their actual `taylor-ft-pNN` anchors. The broader historical intake statements below are superseded where the [current127-unit semantic audit](receipts/T20-whole-semantic-audit-2026-09-08.md) and later T22 receipts record development; that audit’s named whole/consumer debts remain in progress.

Recovery/disposition census complete; canonical writing and source acquisition are separate, unfinished work owned by the parent. This audit changed only this report and its [machine crosswalk](</Users/admin/Central/Work/O-I/Antykathera-Essay-Work/working/p2-enrichment/T20-quilt-to-whole-coverage.json>).

127 recovered rows: 21 inherited wholes, four additional whole scopes, 23 individually retained poems, and 79 subordinate images, source-led research units or explicitly disposed leads. All six quilt programme bodies were read; the corpus register distinguishes full reads from material-section/source backchecks. The count is a recovery result, not a new quota.

Stained glass is independently admitted by the corrected optical programme; its material construction and the atlas’s coordinate discipline remain distinct. The Meal is expressly appointed as the logos-health mythemic body in Q27 and Neumann’s encounter, with the full appetite-to-new-hunger outline recovered. Valentinian Sophia/Horos is a distinct reported whole, not Hypostasis; its proposed founding role remains Offered and its Irenaeus telling needs selected-source recovery. The egg-dream whole-form refraction is retained by the harmonised programme; its actual authorial telling carrier remains missing. World Clock must not fill that gap.

The inherited19 world homes were accepted by the parent; the two ground homes were already settled. The parent reports Neumann/Uroboros drafts and34 reciprocal A/C/E additions installed. This report binds the exact anchors without claiming an independent audit of other active writers’ drafts. Bhairava is anchored to protected NOTES79; Prajāpati/aditi to81. En-Soph and tohubohu retain their distinct occurrences and the parent’s paper-eightfold scope.

**Exact inherited-scope additions:** the Mother whole needs the complete corrected Skenfrith field (wheels, seraphs, eagle valence, Assumption/chiasm, personal rabbit perception). Ares retains bow/lyre/hamartia alongside complete surveillance–account–surety. Atlas retains Blue Marble’s hinge. Mirror retains the complete funhouse/Narcissus and three-motion return already developed in A32. Avatar retains fabricated provenance and the liar’s dividend as a source-gated extension. Taylor’s authored corpus retains every individual poem plus the admitted power/optic/Other images. These are bounded additions, not blanket A-depth restoration.

**Explicitly not ready for whole dispatch:** Biscione’s complete material setting; desert-wanderer’s owning telling; private invariant-I dream’s public/protected choice; private chameleon’s owning story; Jorjani Atlas body-chapter acquisition; the unmined Venus symbolic vector. King/priest/politician is a recovered covenant/mediating-office seam, not an outstanding invented King story. The local Skenfrith/Redbrook/London power trip was expressly excluded as essay history.

## Readiness and authority

Complete census/disposition of materially recovered units across all six quilt programmes and map-owning T20 ledgers. Does not claim all underlying scholarly works acquired, all public tellings verified, all proposed relations written, or parent migration accepted.

The binding corrected map and central-plan amendment admit source-led intake beyond the old21. A source or edition debt does not downgrade the authorial operation. Conversely, a ratified operation does not make a borrowed quotation, historical telling, or new cross-story amplification verified. Each row therefore separates disposition, source debt, development status and exact home. New interpretive edges retain human-amplified:no unless separately authored/ratified.

The 23 poems remain individually addressable within the inherited Taylor corpus. FT-P11–16 preserve the ordered P0–P5 cycle. No poem has been selected for manuscript insertion by this audit. Whole splitting, if needed for length, must preserve those identities and avoid duplicate source bodies.

## Bounded canonical write scopes

These scopes are proposals for the parent’s per-element dispatch. This agent made no canonical changes. Source-house updates and consumer returns require separately bound scopes; protected NOTES are excluded. Already installed ground content should be checked as covered, not rewritten.

- `T20-mytheme-zurvanite-twins`: Quilt good/evil twins materially bear the authorial slash-versus-cancellation operation; core IV supplies Ohrmazd/Ahriman naming.
- `T20-mytheme-paper-erasure`: Erasure changes the mark while its field remains.
- `T20-mytheme-prajapati-hunger`: Genesis and hunger join life, power and food without reducing all creation stories to one.
- `T20-mytheme-aditi-world-eater`: Assimilation and nourishment are distinct from devouring closure.

- `T20-mytheme-eden-serpent`: Retain the authored Eden-serpent relation to the disguised torus without pretending Neumann or Genesis supplies QL notation.
- `T20-mytheme-saturn-devouring`: Monopolising power consumes without giving birth; nourishment must be able to return.
- `T20-mytheme-bhairava-devouring`: Mono-Śiva assimilates Poly-Śakti forms; distinguish liberating assimilation from monopoly.
- `T20-mytheme-hero-centroversion`: The hero’s mental separation and centroversion unfold a transpersonal latency already seeded in the uroboros.
- `T20-mytheme-great-mother-faces`: Preserve binding/releasing and nourishing/terrible faces without simplifying positive/negative to good/evil.
- `T20-mytheme-hieros-gamos-incest`: Self-relation and dissolution precede differentiated generative union.
- `T20-mytheme-rolling-wheel-en-soph`: Self-composition winds about the unobjectified opening.
- `T20-mytheme-horse-sacrifice-world`: The horse stands for the universe in the reported creation/consumption telling.
- `T20-mytheme-mother-child-feeding`: Feeding changes the receiver and joins the Mother/Son return without collapsing traditions.
- `T20-mytheme-tohubohu-light-world`: Illimitable darkness and coming light make distinction and the world-parent field explicit; Anuttara remains distinct from Māyā.

- `T20-mytheme-bohr-taijitu-emblem`: A historical physics emblem participates as reception evidence, distinct from the author’s erased-dot operation.
- `T20-mytheme-centre-everywhere`: Nested real internalities can each be a centre without a final objective circumference.
- `T20-mytheme-fish-water`: Definition’s subject cannot stand outside its sustaining element.
- `T20-mytheme-cage-window`: Definition can enclose its referent or open a return to it.
- `T20-mytheme-kaleidoscope`: A term becomes a local orbital centre and can be decentered; manifold interpretation is not arbitrary.
- `T20-mytheme-necker-cube`: A view flips; context then holds views together without claiming one final reading.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p01`: Here/there and changing play culminate in checkmate and another game.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p02`: Knowability of the unknown and unknowability of the known return as Known/Knower.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p03`: Knowing one and becoming no-one enact recognition.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p04`: Zero, One and All remain in one relational utterance.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p05`: Field/way, sealed/opening, wind and bird return Real as re-All.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p06`: Near/far, old/new and many/few change the seeking relation.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p07`: Humanised spirituality refuses institutions that devour the human.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p08`: Waking confronts tiredness and bullshit in the poem’s own voice.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p09`: Law, window, wind and pigeonhole test freedom’s relation to form.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p10`: Borders, defence and pretence disclose the cost of guarded identity.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p11`: P0: two roads of two; cancellation and 2+4 remain distinct.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p12`: P1: binary limitation returns to shared root and power.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p13`: P2: language fixes and frays; life releases its achieved form.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p14`: P3: one-and-done is answered by the retained relation of two Ones.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p15`: P4: the underlying One question exposes framing, cloud and humility with precision.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p16`: P5: God is context rather than an argumentative object.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p17`: Plain horror and love/fear retain the poem’s affective force.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p18`: Scratching is the itch: desire, capture, child/sin, healing and brothers remain in sequence.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p19`: Beyond consequence is not inconsequential: journey becomes residential.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p20`: The notion born of difference extends the companion consequence poem.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p21`: Infinite multiplied by infinitesimal returns to one sentence and sameness/difference.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p22`: Feeling is undergoing: sea, winds, captain’s boots, Venus/Sophia/Martian figures retain local poetic offices.
- `T20-mytheme-taylor-ft-p23`: Surface/layers, shoe/sole and unreturn preserve the Blake-facing poetic turn.
- `T20-mytheme-god-other`: Finite mind confronts source, law or archetypal totality; the divine-mind reading has its own register.
- `T20-mytheme-shadow-other-self`: Disowned internal difference resists the ego’s completed self-model.
- `T20-mytheme-friend-myselfs-other`: Another centre shares a world while exceeding one psyche’s production of it.
- `T20-mytheme-alien-enemy`: Denied uncertainty is projected into an externalised negative pole.

### T20-mytheme-antikythera-attunement — Antikythera as attunement instrument

**Disposition:** admitted-whole. **Development:** home-settled; recovery-census-complete; canonical development belongs to parent batch.

**Home:** [submission-package/essay/symbolon/mytheme/worlds/frank-taylor/antikythera-attunement/WHOLE.md](</Users/admin/Central/Work/O-I/Antykathera-Essay-Work/submission-package/essay/symbolon/mytheme/worlds/frank-taylor/antikythera-attunement/WHOLE.md>).

**Figures:** Observer; situated dial; gearing; inscriptions; solar/lunar cycles; eclipse/calendar relations; mechanism; sky remaining outside ownership; returned human judgment.
